# Source crosswalk — engineering and execution dispositions, 29 October 2026

Derived from [plan.md](../../plan.md) at `07f8fb81bf4fc97f94c809ec6e510c460b6cf87e` (plan blob `27eb35676300d4bdcc9d6770172671730b04dba0`), replacing the status semantics of prior crosswalk blob `423190e1a51b13352279955b5a1f956ba07b3f76`. This is a derived evidence register, not another roadmap. [Canonical open work](../../BLOCKED_TESTS.md) and [test evidence](TEST_EVIDENCE.md) own continuation.

Status correction, 29 October 2026, source `07f8fb81bf4fc97f94c809ec6e510c460b6cf87e`: all **951** supplied items retain exact source text (**200 original + 190 supplement + 561 research**). The old 948-item `BLOCKED Q` default conflated missing qualification with unfinished engineering. The corrected register records **903 FAIL / engineering OPEN / execution NOT_MAPPED** and **48 externally BLOCKED** with row-specific dependencies. FAIL/open is an evidence-closure queue, **not 903 demonstrated software bugs**. Source/research/applicability work and implementation continue; an unmapped case is not a native or runtime blocker. Known unavailable execution tests remain BLOCKED in the test records and were neither run nor waived. Browser, backend, artifact and native qualification remain separate. No whole-item PASS, unsupported exemption or release-gate qualification was added.

**Read the columns separately.** Overall FAIL with engineering OPEN means implementation, semantic classification, current owner/test/oracle mapping or evidence closure still needs engineering work; it never means that every listed feature is absent. These rows use execution NOT_MAPPED until a concrete per-item case is assigned; lack of that mapping is engineering work, not an external blocker. Only explicit external-only actions/trials receive overall BLOCKED. Preparatory code/source work may continue in their related OPEN rows. A research observation, historical result, illustrative task, optional tool recommendation or policy conflict is preserved as source text and still needs a current canonical mapping; it is not automatically a new feature, proven defect, or authority to replace working systems.

**Execution status:** `NOT_MAPPED` means a concrete per-item current-owner/test/oracle mapping is unfinished. Source, research, applicability review and implementation remain OPEN; this is not a missing-runtime or native-platform finding and does not authorize test execution. The listed indicative lanes are planning hints until an actual case is mapped. `BLOCKED` is reserved here for the 48 explicit external actions/trials with row-specific runner, quota, artifact, consent or owner-approval dependencies. Known unavailable execution suites remain blocked in the canonical test-evidence/work records; this document does not unblock or rerun them. Document-integrity checks are source/document work, not product execution tests. No new access request is made.

**Evidence limits:** `S` marks a relevant source gap recorded at the immutable branch baseline, not a demonstrated exploit or failed live workflow. Current branch source, historical evidence and proposed/uncommitted patch blobs are different evidence classes. This correction grants no deployment, production/staging change, credential rotation, spending, messaging, payment, native-PC access or launch authorization.

## Named execution dependencies

| Code | Exact boundary |
| --- | --- |
| SOURCE | Source/research/applicability work can continue through the authorized repository tools. No executable case or runtime success is inferred from the source text; concrete test/owner mapping remains OPEN. |
| NODE | No connected authorized cloud Node/Vitest/typecheck/build runner with verified free allowance for the exact candidate; standard execution stays blocked. Existing source/static work remains OPEN. |
| PYTHON | No connected authorized cloud CPython runner with verified free allowance for the exact device candidate. Unit authoring, source repair and trace work remain OPEN. |
| DB | Exact-candidate isolated PostgreSQL/PGlite/database-negative execution needs the authorized cloud runtime; existing staging/production mutations are forbidden. SQL/source review remains OPEN. |
| BROWSER | Full exact-candidate hosted Playwright browser/control/viewport/accessibility execution is unavailable/held. Browser frontend/backend implementation and control census remain OPEN; no native OS is required merely to write them. |
| RENDER | Independent document/PDF/spreadsheet/image/video execution needs qualified cloud CPython plus the applicable parser, calculation engine, renderer/codec and output oracle. This does not automatically require licensed Microsoft Office or Windows. |
| WINDOWS | No authorized disposable interactive cloud Windows environment is available for actual UIA/desktop/install/target-race evidence; the personal PC is excluded. |
| OFFICE | The native Microsoft Office path additionally needs legitimate licensed Office in an authorized Windows cloud environment. LibreOffice/file-format evidence cannot qualify that native path. |
| MACOS | Legitimate hosted macOS and its supported permissions/signing environment are unavailable; Linux and browser emulation cannot qualify macOS control. |
| MOBILE | Actual Android emulator/device or legitimate iOS simulator/device execution is unavailable. Responsive browser/PWA coding is a separate OPEN/browser lane. |
| MODEL | Exact-current-model live inference/capacity qualification needs authorized credentials, verified free quota and exact-candidate hosting. Source routing/retry/policy work remains OPEN. |
| HIGH | Live HIGH qualification additionally needs intended free/included inference access and billing eligibility for the exact candidate. Read-only metadata and mocked wiring are not live-model proof. |
| ARTIFACT | The real authorized signed Offline release/private model bytes and scoped R2 access are unavailable. Substitute files cannot qualify real Offline inference; related source work remains OPEN. |
| VOICE | Consented real speech/microphone service and qualified cloud audio execution are unavailable; no personal microphone/PC use is authorized. |
| SIGN | Owner-controlled code-signing/notary identity and the required native cloud build environment are unavailable. Packaging hardening and notice/source review remain OPEN. |
| CONFIG | Necessary external account/infrastructure configuration or staging/production changes need owner authorization; none is granted here. Repository implementation can proceed independently. |
| CREDENTIAL | Rotation/revocation of verified exposed live credentials is owner-controlled and unauthorized here. Never expose/reuse values; source/root-cause mitigation can continue. |
| CONTACT | A real owner-approved support/domain-email/WhatsApp account or contact-channel decision/verification is unavailable; no external message or setup is authorized. |
| PAYMENTS | Merchant/provider/catalogue/legal approval and actual sandbox credentials are unavailable; live payments remain disabled. Independent entitlement/accounting/source readiness work remains OPEN. |
| LEGAL | Authorized owner/professional legal/business review and published-policy sign-off are unavailable. Drafting accurate source/copy and documenting actual data flows remain OPEN. |
| PILOT | Independent consenting new users/pilot participants are unavailable. The agent must not invent users, consent, ratings or contact people without authorization. |
| REVIEW | Final independent qualified security/quality review has no assigned consenting reviewer/evidence. Subagent source review is useful but does not establish that final release gate. |
| COMPETITOR | Comparable legal competitor accounts/configurations, approved free quotas and matched execution environments are unavailable/held. Current primary-document research, task design and oracle implementation remain OPEN. |
| ROLLBACK | An owner-approved disposable deployment/promotion/rollback target and permission are unavailable; existing staging/production infrastructure must not be changed. |
| LAUNCH | Public deployment, main merge, production secrets, spending and payment activation remain owner-controlled and unauthorized. Preparing a release/runbook is independent OPEN work. |

## Release gates

**Current candidate-work evidence, separate from the immutable branch baseline:** the coordinator records RC13 adapted source assertions 61/61 versus baseline 28/61, while all 83 full Vitest cases are UNRUN; debloat header-contract parity is 84 cases / 808 assertions; parser pins 13.0.0 / 6.21.3 are prepared; RC15 direct-command confirmation and STOP/unknown-effect markers are source-only with protocol integration still under review. These limited classes and proposed source changes do not make any whole item PASS and do not prove a current browser/native/provider campaign.

Each gate still contains OPEN engineering/evidence work, so a missing external prerequisite does not hide its unfinished implementation. No gate receives PASS from this document.

Commercial gate index remains **0 / 10 = 0 / 100**. This binary evidence denominator is not the percentage of code written or a measured commercial-readiness percentage. Current-candidate success/recovery rates, p50/p95, cost and competitor results remain **NOT MEASURED**. Existing source repairs and limited adapted-contract assertions must retain their exact evidence class and cannot be promoted to real browser/native/provider qualification.
